# learned: what the learned stage of the tagger decides, for the sentences of
# tools/exam/tests/golden/learned.conllu and the writable sentences of tests/corpus/core, in the readings the
# rules give them. tools/exam/tests/golden.rs writes this file (`make fix-golden`); and
# scripts/train/test_train.py makes the same decisions in Python from the weights file, so a
# change to the file or to the learned stage shows here, and the two must agree.
# A line is a sentence's id, a tab, then each word: the letter of its tag (A for ADJ, in
# alphabetical order of the 13 codes: ADJ ADP ADV AUX CONJ DET INTJ NOUN NUM PART PRON PROPN
# VERB), S, L, U or ? for Sure, Likely, Unsure or Unknown, and `=` with the hex of the tags kept
# (bit n for the nth code) when that is not the tag alone.
# deslag::tag::VERSION 13
# 3384 sentences with 26761 words

s4	KS ML LL ES H?=1081 BL LL=880
s5	KS DS MS BS FS H?=1081 H?
s6	L?=880 ML CL H?=880 HU=1080
